NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
TUESDAY, June 11, 2013  
PRESENT: Dawnafe Whitesinger, Supervisor; David Tenney, Supervisor; Jesse Thompson, Supervisor; and 
Jonathan M. Nez, Chairman were present in person.  
9:00 a.m.  Call to Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation led by Supervisor Whitesinger. 
Chairman Nez asked that we keep Supervisor Allen and her family in our thoughts and prayers as she has lost 
a loved one and will not be present today.  
CALL TO THE PUBLIC: There were no public comments.  
CONSENT AGENDA: a)Minutes May 28, 2013 b) Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000 May 2013 c) Clerk 
of Superior Court Report May 2013 d) Juvenile Probation Report May 2013 e) Justice Court Reports: Holbrook 
Precinct #1, May; Winslow Precinct #2, May; Snowflake Precinct #3, May; Kayenta Precinct #4, May; Show 
Low Precinct #5, May; and Pinetop-Lakeside Precinct #6, May 2013 f) Constable Reports: Winslow Precinct #2 
March 2013 and April 2013; Show Low Precinct #5, May; and Pinetop -Lakeside Precinct #6, May 2013 g) 
Appointment to the Navajo County Republican Committee of the following as Precinct Committeeman to fill an 
existing vac ancy in the respective precinct: Terry Green h) Special Event Liquor License for the Pinetop -
Lakeside Chamber of Commerce 3 rd Annual High Mountain Music Festival i) Amendment to 
Intergovernmental Agreement with TLO Systems for law enforcement access to dat a searches and reports j) 
Amendment to Contract #P10PX76655 for Dispatching Services with Petrified Forest National Park for an 
additional year k) Letter to the Bureau of Justice Assistance in support of the proposed Navajo County Fatality 
Review and Safety Assessment Initiative grant proposal l) Invitation to Secretary of State Ken Bennett to attend 
the Grand Opening and Ribbon Cutting for the new McNary Community Library m) Letter to Coconino 
National Forest Supervisor regarding Four -Forest Restoration In itiative Environmental Impact Statement: 
Kaibab and Coconino #34857 n) Letter of recommendation for Navajo County Supervisor/Chairman Jonathan 
M. Nez regarding an appointment as a candidate as an at -large director to the NACo Board of Directors or a 
Vice-Chair for the Community & Economic Development Steering Committee o) Name an existing road in the 
Snowflake/Taylor area to “Arizona Trail” in Section 31, Township 14 North, Range 23 East : Supervisor 
Tenney made a motion to authorize the Chairman to sign the  items in the consent agenda as  presented; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
      EMPLOYEE RECOGNITION : Navajo County Recognizes Sheila Malone  and Amy Gomez  for their 
excellence in personal performance far exceeding organization expectations : Pam Philli ps recognized Amy 
Gomez and Sheila Malone for their personal performance far exceeding organization expectations. Pam thanked 
each of the employees and honored  them with a certificate  of recognition and a $50 gift card. Chairman Nez 
thanked both employees and indicated that they are an intricate part of the Navajo County family.  
HUMAN RESOURCES:  
a. Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions: Patrick Shinn reviewed the 31 item personnel 
action report. Supervisor Tenney declared a conflict on item number 26. Supervisor Thompson made 
a motion to authorize the Chairman to sign the personnel action report as presented; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Whites inger. Vote unanimous with the exception of Supervisor Tenney  not voting on 
number 26.

<!-- page 2 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 06-11-2013 
Page 2 of 5 
 
b. Personnel Service Awards : Patrick Shinn recognized Norise Whipple, Asher Davis, and Danny Lewis 
for their 5 years of  service to Navajo County;  Howard Hamilton for  his 10 years of service to Navajo 
County; Perry Denton for his 20 years of service to Navajo County.  
      ASSESSOR: Consideration and possible approval of a settlement agreement in Mountain Oaks Apartments v. 
Navajo County in TX -2012000707: Cammy Darris indicated that we are asking for a settlement agreement for 
the Mountain Oaks Apartments. She indicated that this will reduce the 2013 full cash value for the subject 
parcel from $1,380,050 to $870,000.  Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve the settlement agreement 
in Mountain Oaks Apartments v. Navajo County in  case number  TX-2012000707; motion seconded by  
Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
EMERGENCY MANAGEMENT : Update of the current wildfire conditions in Navajo County : Mary 
Springer gave an update of the current wildfire conditions in Navajo County and answe red questions of the 
Board. Mrs. Springer reported  that Stage 2 Restrictions will be  in place beginning on Thursday, June 13  at 
12:01 a.m. Mrs. Springer indicated that this means no campfires and no smoking outside a vehicle.  
NAVAJO COUNTY LIBRARY DISTRICT Board of Directors Session : Supervisor Tenney  made a 
motion to enter into the Navajo County Library District  Board of Directors Session ; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Geneva Durkee is not present at this time. 
Supervisor Tenney made a motion to return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. 
Vote unanimous approving the motion.  Geneva Durkee is now pre sent. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to 
enter into the Navajo County Library District Board of Directors Session ; motion seconded by  Supervisor 
Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
a. CONSENT AGENDA: First Things First grant award to the Librar y District and approve signing the 
agreement to implement a new project to address parent awareness for early literacy development in the 
birth to five year old age group : Geneva Durkee discussed the consent item. Supervisor Tenney made 
a motion to approve the consent agenda as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. 
Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of the acceptance of the two federal Library Services and 
Technology Act grants awarded to the Library District by the Arizona State Library, Archives  and 
Public Records , ‘Reading! We’ll “Dig It” in Navajo County Libraries!’  and ‘ Find Your Library’s 
Geocache and Catch Some Adventure at the Library!’ : Geneva Durkee discussed the item.  Supervisor 
Thompson expressed his appreciation to Geneva for finding many resources to improve our libraries 
and reaching out to our communities.  Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve the acceptance of 
the two federal Library Services and Technology Act grants awarded to th e Library District by the 
Arizona State Library, Archives and Public Records, ‘Reading! We’ll “Dig It” in Na vajo County 
Libraries!’ and ‘ Find Your Library’ s Geocac he and Catch Some Adventure at the Library !’; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
Supervisor Thompson made a motion to return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. 
Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
PUBLIC WORKS: 
a. Consideration and possible approval of Task Agreement N umber 10 to Memorandum of Agreement 
#CTH65T60880 between Bureau of Indian Affairs (BIA), Hopi Agency and Navajo County : Bill Bess 
discussed the item and requested approval indicating that funds have been budgeted in the FY  2012-13 
Budget under District II Special Road  Funds. Supervisor Thompson made a motion  to approve the 
Task Agreement Number 10 to Memorandum of Agreement #CTH65T60880  between Bureau of Indian

<!-- page 3 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 06-11-2013 
Page 3 of 5 
 
Affairs (BIA), Hopi Agency and Navajo County ; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney . Vote 
unanimous approving the motion.  
b. VICTORY HEIGHTS ROAD MAINTENANCE DISTRICT  Board of Directors ’ Session: 
Supervisor Thompson made a motion to enter into the Victory Heights Road Maintenance District  
Board of Directors ’ Session; motion seconded by  Supervisor Whitesinger . Vote unanimous 
approving the motion. Consideration and possible approval of Amendment N umber 1 to the Victory 
Heights Road Maintenance Contract #B12-04-010 with Perkins Cinders, Inc., extending the contract for 
an additional year (June 11, 2013 through J une 10, 2014 ):  Bill Bess discussed the i tem and requested 
approval. Supervisor Tenney made a motion  to approve  the Amendment Number 1 to the Victory 
Heights Road Maintenance Contract #B12-04-010 with Perkins Cinders, Inc., extending the contract for 
an ad ditional year (June 11, 2013 through June 10, 2014); motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to return to 
regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
c. NAVAJO COUNTY FLOOD CONTROL DISTRICT Board of Directors ’ Session: Supervisor 
Tenney made a motion to enter into the Navajo County Flood Control District Board of Directors ’ 
Session; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson . Vote unanimous ap proving the motion. 
Consideration and possible approval of the Intergovernmental Agreement between Navajo County and 
the Town of Taylor for Flood Mitigation : Bill Bess discussed the item. Supervisor Tenney made a  
motion to approve the Intergovernmental Agreement between Navajo County and the Town of Taylor 
for Flood Mitigation ; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger . Vote unanimous approving the 
motion. Supervisor Tenney  made a motion  to return to regular session; mot ion seconded by 
Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.   
d. Consideration and possible approval of an expenditure in an amount not to exceed $40,000 from District 
V Special Road Funds to construct an AC Milling Multi Use Path on Porter Moun tain Road to be used 
by Blue Ridge Junior High Students : Bill Bess discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger made a  
motion to approve an expenditure in an amount not to exceed $40,000 from District V Special Road 
Funds to construct an AC Milling Multi Use  Path on Porter Mountain Road to be used by Blue Ridge 
Junior High Students; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
e. PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 17-13, approving a 
Special Use Permit for Cellular One to place a cellular communications tower, with a height not to 
exceed 180 feet, on Assessor’s Parcel Number 107 -07-046F: Trent Larson introduced Peggy Saunders, 
Senior Secretary with Planning and Z oning. Mr. Larson discussed the item. Chairman Nez opened the 
Public H earing and inquired  if there were any written comments  received. There were no written 
comments received. Mr. Larson indicated Mr. Dennis Baker representative from Cellular One was 
present. Mr. Baker made comments to the Board. Chairman Nez closed the Public Hearing. Supervisor 
Tenney made a motion to adopt  Resolution Number 17 -13, approving a Special Use Permit for 
Cellular One to place a cellular communications tower, with a height not to exceed 180 feet, on  
Assessor’s Parcel Number 107 -07-046F; motion seconded by  Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.  
f. PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 18-13, approving a 
Special Use Permit for Cellular One to place a cellular communications tower, with a height not to 
exceed 180 feet, on Assessor’s Parcel Number 212 -20-147A: Trent Larson discussed the item. 
Chairman Nez opened the  Public Hearing. Chairman Nez  indicated tha t there was one written 
comment in opposition to the Resolution that has been noted. There being no comments, Chairman Nez 
closed the P ublic Hearing. Supervisor Whitesinger thanked Mr. Larson for taking the time to collect 
the information and in working with the community  there. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion  to

<!-- page 4 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 06-11-2013 
Page 4 of 5 
 
adopt Resolution Number 18 -13, approving a Special Use Permit for Cellular One to place a cellular 
communications tower, with a height not to exceed 180 feet, on Asses sor’s Parcel  Number 212 -20-
147A; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
COUNTY SUPERVISORS ASSOCIATION: Presentation in regard to the activities of the County 
Supervisors Association, including a discussion of the recent legislativ e session (Craig Sullivan/Hunter Moore) 
This item was pulled.  
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE : Report from Board members, County Attorney and County Manager : 
Supervisor Whitesinger reported on the  First Things First  meeting where she serves on the White Mountain  
Apache Regional Partnership Council. She stated she was appointed Vice President of this Council for the third 
consecutive year. She discussed attending the White Mountain Apache Tribal meeting with Sheriff Clark which 
addressed the Cross Commissioning Agreement. She indicated she met with the Directors of the local Chambers 
of C ommerce in developing relationship s with the Tribe and addressed what the  White Mountain  Tribal 
Community has to offer in the realm of tourism  to summer visitors. She indicated she took some members of 
Navajo County to show  off the beauty of the  White Mountain Apache Tribe  area. She  related she  had the 
opportunity to speak with some 8th grade students where she  spoke on  hard work with  the idea of being 
successful, the i mportance of smiling, always having  a good attitude and to be good to others. Supervisor 
Tenney reported on his daughter ’s marriage. He further reported on the L egislature meetings regarding the 
budget and  reported on CSA’s establishing the priorities  to help the counties  budgets. Supervisor Tenney  
expressed his condolences to Supervisor Allen and her family  for their loss of her son -in-law and stated h is 
heart and prayers go out to them. Supervisor Thompson  expressed his condolences to Supervisor Alle n. 
Supervisor Thompson  stated he was sad to report  that another Code T alker, Milton G ishal from Jeddito 
Chapter passed away on Saturday. He expressed appreciation to Mr. Jayne for attending the Indian Wells Senior 
Center Grand O pening where the  Senior C enter is moving to a new location . He reported attending the  
Memorial Day cer emony at Leupp  and stated he also  attended the Spider Mound event of  a new building 
opening where Hopi Vice Chairman Honanie  and other Hopi Tribal department leaders  attended. Supervisor 
Thompson announced that the Dilk on Road Y ard will hold  an upcoming  heavy equipment road operator’s 
certification training, which is the first training of its kind . James Jayne expressed appreciation to Supervisor 
Thompson for his words. Brad Carlyon thanked Supervisor Whitesinger for the trip to Cibeque Creek in  the 
White Mountains where he enjoyed the rock climbing and the walk, the pool under the waterfall and  seeing the 
beauty of the area . Mr. Carlyon  reported that he, Judge Higgins and Sherif f Clark sponsored an Elder Abuse 
Seminar in Winslow where he indicated about 75 people attended and reported it was a successful meeting. He 
also stated that he attended the County Leadership Institute sponsored by NACO which was a very enlightening 
experience and on a personal note  indicated his son finished his hunter safety course and received the highest 
score of the youth for the test  and is ready for the elk hunt this fall . Chairman Nez reported on a meeting in 
Kayenta where Sheriff Clark participated in a community based effort to combat drug trafficking and gangs . He 
expressed his appreciation to the Sheriff’s department for participating. He reported on road projects that are 
underway in District I which will greatly improve the quality of life to his communities and indicated the people 
are very grateful and appreciative of the County’s support. He indicated he will be attending the funeral for the 
son of the Kayenta Chapter President and asked for prayers for them in their  time of loss. Chairman Nez also 
reported taking his  family to the Grand Canyon  on the Navajo Nation side and showed the picture of Blue 
Springs where the Colorado River and Blue Springs  merges. Supervisor Thompson reported he helped gather 
200 plus wild horses which were taken to Window Rock. 
 
Navajo word of the Day:   T’    koh - meaning okay, I will go along.

ADJOURN: At 10:09 a.m.  Supervisor Thompson  made a motion to adjourn; motion seconded by  
Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
WORK SESSION/LUNCHEON: Cancelled 
 
 
 
 
APPROVED: 
 
Jonathan M. Nez, Chairman 
 
ATTEST:  
 
 
 
 
DATE:  June 25, 2013 
 
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board
